# EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md

Append-only index of major experiments and decisions. Keep entries short and
link to detailed logs. Do not use this as the only source for metrics; verify
from the linked session/data files before making claims.

## Current Next Experiment Candidate

**Active pivot (2026-05-21)**: Track A P7/Branch B normalized cube grasp primitive, now corrected against v4/v5/v6/v7 B200 logs and the 5/21 compliance-direction analysis. Current evidence says rigid offset variants can pass static/prep but fail close-time dynamics through one-sided push. v7 is static/prep-valid and USD-converted under the D024 B200 override path, but approved close_26 telemetry failed; material-only soft contact failed; approved virtual compression+damping also failed posthoc despite fixing step-3 speed and steps 2-4 one-sided push. Static attribution now says the missing pieces are target-error control below 3mm and support/damping retention beyond step 4. The next Track A mechanism shape is target-guarded micro-close plus support-horizon damping. Track B OpenVLA-OFT/pi0 work is separate but creates a B200 endgame preservation requirement. Any further Track A runtime still requires separate approval.

Recommended next experiment:

1. Treat v7 as static/prep-valid, D024 USD-converted, and close_26 runtime-telemetry failed. Do not claim physics validation.
2. Default next Track A work is default-off target-guarded micro-close plus support-horizon damping design; keep fixed audit gates unchanged.
3. Before Track B heavy training or B200 release, preserve Track A `/tmp` logs and code/doc artifacts.
4. Do not run hold-lift; close-time contact/dynamics failed.
5. Any new Track A runtime gate needs separate approval and must remain close_26-only first.
6. Do not train, generate cube sim datasets, integrate constraints/defaults, attach SurfaceGripper, transport, release, or tune P7 scalar/gates in Track A.

FK/IK/env all use `link5_to_tcp=[0,0,0.115428]`, and Candidate6 built-in DiffIK subtracts the rotated TCP offset before commanding the link5 body. So IK is not solving for bare link5 origin. Existing telemetry also reports `actual_fk_vs_sim_tcp_err_mm_final=0.0`. The narrower geometry issue is real: `hand_tcp` local z is `0.115428`, while link5 collision AABB local `z_max=0.11988562011718751`, so the distal collision surface is about `4.46mm` beyond the hand_tcp point. Existing x240 logs split the old shortfall into applied FK shortfall about `3.46mm` plus applied-to-actual lag about `5.6mm`, not one missing TCP offset. | Verdict `TCP_OFFSET_PRESENT_CONTACT_PROXY_GEOMETRY_MATTERS`.
